# WARWIKI literature inbox — 2026-09-11

Search window: **2026-06-28 through 2026-09-11**. 1212 deduplicated records.

**Screening candidates, not verified clinical updates.** Publication-type labels may lag indexing; priority signals do not establish study quality. Read the paper, verify dates and outcomes, check corrections/retractions, and compare the current page before editing. The overlap intentionally includes older papers indexed recently. Abstracts and full text are not republished here.

## Search coverage

- urethral-reconstruction: 104 records; query: `((TITLE:"urethroplasty" OR ABSTRACT:"urethroplasty") OR (TITLE:"urethral stricture" OR ABSTRACT:"urethral stricture") OR (TITLE:"urethral reconstruction" OR ABSTRACT:"urethral reconstruction")) AND SRC:MED AND FIRST_PDATE:[1900-01-01 TO 2026-09-11] AND (FIRST_PDATE:[2026-06-28 TO 2026-09-11] OR FIRST_IDATE:[2026-06-28 TO 2026-09-11])`
- upper-tract-reconstruction: 93 records; query: `((TITLE:"ureteroplasty" OR ABSTRACT:"ureteroplasty") OR (TITLE:"ureteral reconstruction" OR ABSTRACT:"ureteral reconstruction") OR (TITLE:"ureteral stricture" OR ABSTRACT:"ureteral stricture") OR (TITLE:"pyeloplasty" OR ABSTRACT:"pyeloplasty") OR (TITLE:"ileal ureter" OR ABSTRACT:"ileal ureter")) AND SRC:MED AND FIRST_PDATE:[1900-01-01 TO 2026-09-11] AND (FIRST_PDATE:[2026-06-28 TO 2026-09-11] OR FIRST_IDATE:[2026-06-28 TO 2026-09-11])`
